## Coverage limits

Forward inference precedents only; explicit backward/training sources are excluded.
Context-parallel coverage concerns partial-result normalization; cross-rank transport is not extracted.
Gated-delta and compression have distinct semantics; softmax cards do not apply to them by default.
Native OPUS source is classified as HIP/C++; ASM records describe launch contracts, not disassembled instructions.
Tuned attention databases and new GPU measurements are not included.
